1. LSE/L-BFGS on unnormalized log variables with penalty enforcing max≤1 and maximizing sum. We can write objective `penalized_objective(x, tau, lam, target=1)`: b=exp(clipped x). c=convolve_fft(b). Define:
```
def obj_lse(x, tau, n):
    a = softmax(x)
    c = convolution(a)
    cmax = c.max()
    vals = np.exp((c-cmax)/tau)
    return cmax + tau*np.log(vals.sum())
def grad_lse(x, tau, n):
    a=softmax(x)
    c=...
    cmax=...
    w=...
    w/=w.sum()
    A = rfft(a,L)
    W = rfft(w,L)  # w length Lc, pad to L
    h = irfft(np.conj(A)*W, L)[:n]  # cross corr
    ga=2*h.real
    return a*(ga - np.dot(a,ga))
```

def lbfgs_optimize(a0, n, durations):
  x=np.log(a0+1e-300)
  best=a0,best_score=score_internal
  for beta in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]:
    tau=beta*score_internal(a)[1]/(2n)? cmax
    res=minimize(obj_lse, x,args=(tau,n),jac=grad_lse, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':...,'maxfun':...})
    x=res.x
    a=softmax(x)
    update best
  return best
```

Helper functions top-level:

```
def _softmax(x):
    x = x - np.max(x)
    # protect under/overflow
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = ex.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return ex/s
```

def _next_pow2(m):
   return 1 << int(np.ceil(np.log2(m))) maybe for int.

```
def convolution_fft(a):
    n=len(a); L= _next_pow2(2*n-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    # small negative due to roundoff: c = np.maximum(c,0) maybe
    c = np.where(c>0,c,0.0)
    return c
```

Internal score:
```
def internal_score(a, do_clip=False):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return inf,0,0
    a_norm=a/s  # or if unnormalized?
    c=convolution(a_norm)
    m=c.max()
    return 2*len(a)*m, m, s
```

Gradient args:
```
def gradient_args(x, tau, n):
   a=softmax(x)
   c=convolution_fft(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
   w=np.exp(shifted)
   sw=w.sum()
   if sw==0: w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
   else: w/=sw
   L=conv_len(n)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W, L)[:n]
   ga=2*h.real
   ga[np.abs(ga)<1e-15]=0
   dot=np.dot(a,ga)
   gx=a*(ga-dot)
   gx[~np.isfinite]=0
   return gx
```
Check gradient sign: LSE increasing with c. To minimize, gradient positive for variables contributing to active. In log param formula, `a*(ga - dot)` may have signs; if dot positive, some coordinates negative? Let's verify for uniform a, c triangular; active central? It should point to reduce peaks. Good.

Potential issue: If tau small, w concentrated at max, dot = a·ga =? ga = grad of c_max approx 2(a active conv). dot = ∑ a_i grad c_max_i = 2 c_max? Since ∑ a_i ∂c_k/∂a_i =2c_k for homogeneous? Yes dot=2cmax? Then `ga - dot*a_i` can be negative/positive. This is gradient on log simplex; okay.

We can scale tau by cmax, but gradient using tau small. Good.

```
b=np.empty(n); b[0]=1.0
for i in range(1,n): b[i]=b[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
return b

Let's define `deconvolve(a, max_time, verbose=False)` top-level, returns best normalized a. It works on unnormalized b. It can take current best and try multiple regularization/alphas. Use while time.

Pseudo:
```
def deconvolve(a, max_time, reg_list=None):
    a=np.array(a,float); s=a.sum(); if s==0: a ones/n; s=1/a? 
    c=convolution_fft(a); maxc=c.max()
    if maxc<=0: return a/s
    b=a/np.sqrt(maxc)  # max=1
    best_b=b.copy(); best_score=score_unnorm(b)
    deadline=...
    iter=0
    while now<deadline:
        c=convolution_fft(best_b); maxc=c.max(); if maxc<...
        b=best_b/np.sqrt(maxc)
        R=1.0 - c/maxc? If rescale, use c_new=c/maxc.
        # maybe if R.sum() <= 1e-10: break
        L=exp
        B=rfft(b,L)
        # R length Lc, pad
        Rfull=np.zeros(L); Rfull[:len(R)]=R.real
        Rf=rfft(Rfull,L)
        denom=np.abs(B)**2
        for reg in [1e-10,1e-8,1e-6,1e-4] times max(denom):
            Qf=0.5 * np.conj(B)*Rf / (denom + reg)
            q=irfft(Qf,L)[:n]
            # try alpha values
            for alpha in [0.5,0.2,0.1,0.5?]:
                b2=b + alpha*q
                b2=np.maximum(b2,0)
                if b2.sum()<=0: continue
                c2=conv(b2); m2=c2.max()
                if m2>0: b2/=np.sqrt(m2)
                score2=2n/(b2.sum()**2)
                if score2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                     best_b=b2; best_score=score2; improved=True
                     break
        if not improved: maybe break or reduce reg
    return best_b/best_b.sum()
```

```
def subgradient_update(a, weight_tau, step):
   c=conv(a); M=c.max()
   tau=weight_tau*M
   if tau>0: w=softmax(c,tau) else top
   ga=2*cross_corr(w,a)
   # maybe normalize ga by norm
   a2=a - step*ga
   a2=np.maximum(a2,0); s=a2.sum(); if s>0: a2/=s
   return a2
```

Resampling existing to n:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
    m=len(seq)
    if m==n: return seq
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq)
    out=np.maximum(out,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out/=s
    return out
```
Interpolation may smooth and lose local structure; okay. Also maybe use max-pool? No.

Power-law seeds: We can generate with exponents. But central is special. We can define:
```
def power_seed(n, alpha, beta):
   i=np.arange(n)
   x=np.power(i+1, -alpha)*np.power(n-i, beta) # n-i >=1
   x=np.maximum(x,1e-12)
   return x/x.sum()
```

```

Use `len_cache` keyed by n.
```
def fft_len(n):
    if n not in _LEN_CACHE:
       m=2*n-1
       L=1 << (m-1).bit_length()
       _LEN_CACHE[n]=L
    return _LEN_CACHE[n]
```
If m=1? n>=1.

```
def convolution(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 512:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       L=fft_len(n)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
       c=c[:2*n-1]
       # eliminate tiny negative
       c[c < 0] = 0.0
    return c
```
Should direct for n<=512 exact and faster. For gradient cross, we always use FFT, can also direct with np.correlate for n<=256? But use FFT for simplicity.

```
def cross_convolution(w, a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 256:
       # w longer; use np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid')? Need orientation. Let's test np.correlate(w,a,'valid') returns ∑ w[i+j] a[j]? I think yes. To avoid uncertainty, use FFT for all.
    L=fft_len(n) # L>=2n-1
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
    h = h.real
    return 2*h
```
We can use `fft_len(n)` for cross; okay.

def eval_internal(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   s=a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-15: return np.inf, 0
   a=a/s
   c=convolution(a)
   m=c.max() if c.size else 0
   return 2*len(a)*m, m
```

```
def score_unnormalized(b):
   s=b.sum()
   if s<=0: return inf
   c=convolution(b)
   m=c.max()
   if m<=0: return inf
   return 2*len(b)*m/(s*s)
```
For deconv after b max=1, can use m but call general.

`scale_max_one(b)`:
```
def scale_max_one(b):
   c=convolution(b); m=c.max()
   if m<=0: return b.copy(), 1.0, c
   return b/np.sqrt(m), 1.0, c/m
```
Return scaled c too to avoid recomputing.

`regular_step(b, c1, target, reg_frac, L)`:
```
def deconv_step_arrays(b, c1, target, reg_frac):
   n=len(b); L=fft_len(n)
   R = target - c1
   # R length 2n-1; if L > lenR, rfft handles pad.
   B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
   Rf = np.fft.rfft(R, L)
   denom = np.abs(B)**2
   reg = reg_frac * max(1e-30, float(np.max(denom)))
   Qf = 0.5 * np.conj(B) * Rf / (denom + reg)
   q = np.fft.irfft(Qf, L)[:n]
   q = q.real
   q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0.0
   return q
```
Note if L from fft_len(n) >=2n-1, `rfft(R,L)` pads. If R length 2n-1 exactly, okay.

Deconvolve main:
```
def deconvolve(a, max_time):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: a=np.ones(n)/n; s=1
   a=a/s
   b0, c0, _ = scale... Actually score normalized: We can start unnormalized `b=a/np.sqrt(m)` using m from convolution.
   c=convolution(a); m=c.max(); if m<=0: return a
   b=a/np.sqrt(m)
   best_b=b.copy(); best_score=score_unnormalized(b) # should 2n/s^2
   if not finite return
   deadline=...
   targets=[1.0,1.05,0.95,1.02,0.98]
   reg_fracs=[1e-10,1e-8,1e-6]
   deltas=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]
   ti=ri=0; stall=0
   L=fft_len(n)
   while time<deadline:
       b1, c1, m1 = scale_max_one(best_b)  # best_b maybe not max1
       target=targets[ti%len]; reg=reg_fracs[ri%len]
       q=deconv_step_arrays(b1, c1, target, reg)
       qmax=np.max(np.abs(q))
       if not finite or qmax<1e-15: stall++; ti+=1; continue
       best_step=None; best_sc=best_score
       for delta in deltas:
           alpha=delta/qmax
           b2=b1 + alpha*q
           # Optionally clip negative; if delta large, many negative; maybe try only top updates?
           b2=np.maximum(b2, 0.0)
           if b2.sum()<=0: continue
           sc=score_unnormalized(b2)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_sc=sc; best_step=b2.copy()
       if best_step is not None:
           # optionally try reverse sign? If q sum negative, maybe -q improves? Try a few negative deltas too
           for delta in [0.02,0.05,0.1]:
               alpha=delta/qmax
               b2=np.maximum(b1 - alpha*q,0)
               sc=...
           if improved update; stall=0
       else:
           stall+=1
       if stall>=6: break
       ti += 1
       ri += (1 if stall==0 else 0)
```
Try reverse sign: If solving target-flat gives q pointing to uniform (maybe increases sum), negative might raise active? Could help shape. But if no improvement, maybe opposite. Include few.
Need be careful if δ=0.5 with qmax leads to large changes; positive and negative. Could use `b2 = b1 + alpha*q`; if many negative clipped, okay. To avoid zeroing many, could limit delta to 0.2.

After deconvolve, normalize `best_b/best_b.sum()` and return. The best_score returned internal; maybe also return score? We can update external.

L-BFGS: Use objective args with `tau` absolute. But for normalized a, cmax ~score/(2n). Choose beta list; `tau=beta*max(cmax,1e-30)`. For exact max, beta small. However, objective `lse(c)` not subtracting `cmax`; gradient scales. If tau very small, LSE may be ill-conditioned due exp differences; `c-cmax` can be -1e-16 etc. Fine.

```
def smoothed_objective(x, tau):
   a=softmax(x)
   c=convolution(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   if cmax <=0: return 0.0
   shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
   sh=x; exp=np.exp(shifted)
   sume=exp.sum()
   if sume<=0 or not finite: return 1e100
   return cmax + tau * np.log(sume) - np.log(len(c))
```
Subtract `np.log(len(c))` optional; constant, okay. If tau=0? not. Use tau>0.

```
def smoothed_gradient(x, tau):
   a=softmax(x)
   c=convolution(a)
   cmax=c.max()
   shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
   w=np.exp(shifted)
   sume=w.sum()
   if sume<=0: w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
   else: w/=sume
   ga=cross_convolution(w,a)  # includes factor2
   dot=np.dot(a,ga)
   gx=a*(ga-dot)
   gx[~np.isfinite(gx)]=0
   return gx
```
Potential scaling: If we subtract log(len(c)) constant, gradient unaffected.

Run L-BFGS:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, max_time, beta_schedule=None):
   n=len(a); a normalized
   x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   x = x - np.mean(x)  # gauge
   best_score, _ = eval_internal(a)
   best_a=a.copy()
   if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule=[0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]
   deadline=...
   for beta in beta_schedule:
       if time>deadline break
       sc,cmax=eval_internal(best_a)
       if sc==inf: break
       tau=max(beta*cmax, 1e-12)
       # Determine maxiter based on n: for n small allow more
       max_iter = 800 if n<=6000 else 400 if n<=12000 else 250
       maxfun = max_iter*3
       # time subbudget per beta maybe 60s? Use options and break? L-BFGS no callback. We can set generous and after return check time.
       try:
          res=minimize(smoothed_objective, x, args=(tau,), jac=smoothed_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max_iter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-11,'maxls':50})
          if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)): x=res.x
       except Exception: pass
       a=softmax(x)
       sc,_=eval_internal(a)
       if sc<best_score: update
       # Also try small perturbation? no
   return best_a
```
Since no callback, a single beta could exceed time (e.g., maxfun 2400 evals * 5ms=12s; okay). For n=4806 2400 evals maybe 5s. 10 beta 50s. Good. For n=16384 250*3=750 evals *10ms=7.5s per beta, 10 beta 75s. Good. We can allocate.

But L-BFGS with `maxfun` counts fun evaluations; jac called extra. If too many, time maybe 2x. Still okay.

We can also run L-BFGS from deconv best with smaller betas only, to refine. Maybe `run_lbfgs` called twice; total time.

Subgrad polish final (optional quick):
```
def polish_small(a, max_time):
   n=len(a); a normalized
   best=a; best_score=eval_internal
   deadline=...
   eta=0.05
   eps_list=[0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0]
   for eps in eps_list:
      eta=0.05
      for _ in range(200):
         if time>deadline break
         c=convolution(a); M=c.max()
         if M<=0: break
         if eps>0:
            tau=eps*M
            w=softmax_c(c,tau)
         else:
            # top 1% constraints
            thresh=np.percentile(c,99)
            w=np.where(c>=thresh,1.0,0.0); w/=w.sum()
         ga=cross_convolution(w,a)
         gmax=np.max(np.abs(ga))
         if gmax<=0: break
         improved=False
         for mult in [1.0,0.5,0.2,0.05]:
            step=eta*mult
            # scale step such that max change ~ step/gmax? previous used absolute; let's use normalized: a2 = a - (step/gmax)*ga? If gmax ~1/n, step/gmax large? Let's use absolute `eta` and if change to large adapt.
            a2=a - step*ga
            a2=np.maximum(a2,0); s=a2.sum()
            if s<=0: continue
            a2/=s
            sc,_=eval_internal(a2)
            if sc<best_score:
                best=a2; best_score=sc; a=a2; improved=True; break
         if not improved:
            eta*=0.5
            if eta<1e-6: break
   return best
```
But if `ga` ~1e-4, step=0.05 gives change 5e-6; okay. If n smaller, ga bigger, step maybe too large; line search handles maybe decrease. Could normalize: `step = eta / max(gmax, 1e-15)` gives max change eta, i.e., coordinate can change by O(eta). That may be too large (eta 0.05). But line search can lower. Use normalized to be scale-independent: `a2 = a - (eta/gmax)*ga`; max change <=eta. Try eta in [0.01,0.005,...]. Since a_i ~1/n, changing 0.01 is huge (relative n). For n=4000, a_i 2e-4, 0.01 is 50000%. Too large. We need step relative to a scale. Previous absolute step=0.03 with ga~2e-4 gave change 6e-6 (3%). To generalize, set `max_change = 0.01 / np.sqrt(n)`? Or target relative change 5% of typical a ~1/n: change ~0.05/n. If gmax ~constant/n, step = (0.05/n)/gmax = 0.05/constant ~0.1. So normalized max_change 0.05/n, not 0.05. Thus line search with `step = change_scale * (1.0/mask?)`.
Compute `typical = max(1.0/n, np.mean(a))`; `target_change = 0.05*typical`; `eta = target_change/gmax`. Then changes ~5% typical. Use `change_scale` schedule [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.001]. Good.

Now seeds. `central_sequence` normalized:
```
def central_sequence(n):
   b=np.empty(n); b[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): b[i]=b[i-1]*(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
   s=b.sum()
   return b/s
```
For n=1? handle.

Let's examine mathematical correctness of L-BFGS gradient with softmax and lse. Suppose x shift: softmax invariant, gradient wrt x should be zero along constant direction. Our formula: `a*(ga - dot)` where dot=∑a_i ga_i. Sum over i = ga - dot times a? Sum_i a_i(ga_i-dot)=∑a_i ga_i - dot∑a_i=0. Good. Shift direction zero. L-BFGS may have flat, but okay.

Pseudo `optimize_length`:
```
def optimize_length(n, max_time, base_seq=None):
    if max_time < 20: return central_sequence(n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max_time
    # seeds
    seeds=[]
    sc, _ = eval_internal(central_sequence(n)); seeds.append(central)
    if base_seq is not None:
       b=resample(base_seq,n)
       seeds.append(b)
    # maybe reversed central for diversity if time
    if max_time > 600:
       rc=central_sequence(n)[::-1]; seeds.append(rc/sum)
    # screening: short deconv for each seed
    screened=[]
    screen_deadline=min(deadline - max_time*0.7, start+ max(30, max_time*0.15))
    for seed in seeds:
       if time>screen_deadline: cand=seed
       else:
           t=max(5, min(90, (screen_deadline-now)//len(remaining seeds)?))
           cand=deconvolve(seed, t)
       sc,_=eval_internal(cand); screened.append((sc,cand))
    # choose best 1 or 2 to run full? If time allows, run full chain on top 2 seeds.
    screened.sort(reverse)
    # time left
    def budget... no closures; compute list
    chains = [(score, seed) for top]
    # If total time large, top 2 else 1.
    num_chains = 2 if max_time > 800 and len(screened)>=2 else 1
    time_per_chain = max(60, (deadline - time) // num_chains)
    for score, seed in chains[:num_chains]:
       # Stage 1 L-BFGS 50%
       t1 = min(stage_fraction, deadline-now)
       a = run_lbfgs(seed, t1)
       # Stage 2 deconv 20%
       a = deconvolve(a, t2)
       # Stage 3 L-BFGS low 15%
       a = run_lbfgs(a, t3, low_betas)
       # Stage 4 polish 15%
       a = polish(a, t4)
       sc=eval; update best
       if time>deadline: break
    return best
```
No nested functions; but computing top sort okay.

def run_lbfgs(a, max_time, beta_list=None):

def polish(a, max_time):
    if max_time < 10: return a
    a=normalize
    best_score,_=eval_internal(a); best=a.copy()
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_time
    eps_list=[0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0]
    for eps in eps_list:
       if time>deadline break
       change_list=[0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
       current=a
       for _ in range(50):
          c=convolution(current); M=c.max()
          if M<=0: break
          if eps>0:
             tau=eps*M
             w=softmax_c(c,tau)
          else:
             # active top 2%
             thresh=np.percentile(c, 98)
             w=(c>=thresh).astype(float)
             ws=w.sum(); if ws>0: w/=ws
          ga=cross_convolution(w,current)
          gmax=np.max(np.abs(ga))
          if gmax<=0: break
          typical=max(1.0/len(current), np.mean(current))
          improved=False
          for change in change_list:
              step=change*typical/gmax
              a2=current - step*ga
              a2=np.maximum(a2,0)
              s=a2.sum()
              if s<=0: continue
              a2/=s
              sc=_=eval_internal(a2)
              if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                  best_score=sc; best=a2.copy(); current=a2; improved=True
                  break
          if not improved:
              # try smaller changes? scale list already; break
              break
          if time>deadline: break
    return best
```

Let's write code carefully.

```
_CONVLEN_CACHE = {}
def _conv_len(n):
    L = _CONVLEN_CACHE.get(n)
    if L is None:
        m = 2*n - 1
        L = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
        _CONVLEN_CACHE[n] = L
    return L
```

`softmax`:
```
def softmax(x):
   x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
   xm = x.max()
   if not np.isfinite(xm): return np.ones_like(x)/len(x)
   e = np.exp(x - xm)
   s = e.sum()
   if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
      return np.ones_like(x)/len(x)
   return e / s
```

`convolution` direct for n<=512; else FFT. Ensure input float.
```
def convolution(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0)
    if n <= 512:
       c = np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
       L=_conv_len(n)
       fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)
       c=c[:2*n-1]
       c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c
```
Rounding negative set zero; if true positive small negative? no.

`cross_convolution` returns 2corr.
```
def cross_convolution(w, a):
    n=len(a); L=_conv_len(n)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*W,L)[:n]
    h=np.nan_to_num(h.real, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return 2.0*h
```
If `w` length >L? L>=2n-1=len(w), okay.

`normalize_sequence`:
```
def normalize_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones_like(a)/len(a)
   return a/s
```

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if len(a)==0: return np.inf, 0.0
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15: return np.inf,0
   a=a/s
   c=convolution(a)
   m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
   return 2.0*len(a)*m, m
```

`score_unnormalized` as above.

`scale_max_one`:
```
def scale_max_one(b):
   b=np.asarray(b,float)
   c=convolution(b)
   m=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
   if m <= 1e-300:
      return b.copy(), 1.0, np.zeros_like(c)
   return b/np.sqrt(m), 1.0, c/np.sqrt(m)? Wait c/m not c/sqrt(m). Score max: If b1=b/√m, c1=c/m. Yes return c/m.
```
Because m scales quadratically. Use `c/m`.

`deconv_step`:
```
def deconv_step(b, c_current_scaled, target, reg_frac):
    n=len(b)
    L=_conv_len(n)
    R = target - c_current_scaled
    R = np.nan_to_num(R, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    Rf = np.fft.rfft(R, L)
    denom = np.abs(B)**2
    maxdenom = float(np.max(denom)) if denom.size else 1.0
    if maxdenom <= 0: maxdenom=1.0
    reg = reg_frac * maxdenom
    Qf = 0.5 * np.conj(B) * Rf / (denom + reg)
    q = np.fft.irfft(Qf, L)[:n]
    q = np.nan_to_num(q.real, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return q
```

`deconvolve`:
```
def deconvolve(a, max_time):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    n=len(a)
    if n==0 or max_time <=0: return a
    c=convolution(a); m=float(c.max())
    if m<=0: return a
    b=a/np.sqrt(m)
    best_b=b.copy()
    best_score=score_unnormalized(b)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score): return a
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_time
    targets=[1.0, 1.05, 0.95, 1.02, 0.98]
    reg_fracs=[1e-9, 1e-7, 1e-5]
    deltas=[0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4]
    ti=ri=0; stall=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b1, _, c1 = scale_max_one(best_b)
        target=targets[ti % len(targets)]
        reg=reg_fracs[ri % len(reg_fracs)]
        q=deconv_step(b1, c1, target, reg)
        qmax=float(np.max(np.abs(q)))
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 1e-15:
            ti += 1; continue
        best_sc=best_score; best_cand=None
        signs = [1, -1] if stall>1 else [1]
        for sign in signs:
            for delta in deltas:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
                alpha = (sign * delta) / qmax
                b2 = b1 + alpha * q
                # clip tiny negative? use maximum
                if np.any(b2 < 0):
                    b2 = np.maximum(b2, 0.0)
                if b2.size ==0: continue
                if float(b2.sum()) <= 0: continue
                if float(np.max(b2)) > 10.0: continue
                sc = score_unnormalized(b2)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc; best_cand = b2.copy()
        if best_cand is not None:
            best_b = best_cand
            best_score = best_sc
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 5: break
        ti += 1
        if stall == 0 and ri % 2 == 1:
            ri += 1
    out = normalize_sequence(best_b)
    return out
```
Note if negative sign with alpha negative, b2 = b1 - delta/qmax q. Good. `np.max(b2)>10` may occur if q large; okay. If b2 all zeros skip.

Potential issue: `b2 = b1 + alpha*q` where alpha negative may still be fine. `if np.any(b2<0)` check each candidate O(n), okay. Could skip.

`central_sequence` loop. For n large, Python loop 12k okay.
```
def central_sequence(n):
    b=np.ones(n)
    if n>1:
       b[0]=1.0
       for i in range(1,n): b[i]=b[i-1]*(2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i)
    return normalize_sequence(b)
```

`resample_sequence` if base None.
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    seq=np.asarray(seq,float)
    if seq.size == n: out=seq.copy()
    else:
       # If small/large, linear interp in [0,1]
       m=seq.size
       if m <=0: out=np.ones(n)
       else:
          x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
          x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
          out=np.interp(x_new,x_old,seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out)
```

`smoothed_objective(x, tau)`:
```
def smoothed_objective(x, tau):
    a = softmax(x)
    c = convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0: return 0.0
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0: return 0.0
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s): return 1e100
    return cmax + tau * np.log(s) - np.log(float(c.size))
```

`smoothed_gradient(x, tau)`:
```
def smoothed_gradient(x, tau):
   a=softmax(x); c=convolution(a); cmax=c.max()
   shifted=(c-cmax)/tau; w=np.exp(shifted); s=w.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: w=np.ones_like(c)/c.size
   else: w=w/s
   ga=cross_convolution(w,a)
   dot=float(np.dot(a,ga))
   gx=a*(ga-dot)
   gx=np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   return gx
```
If tau extremely small, c-cmax may have negative large, exp underflow fine. If cmax 0 return zeros.

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, max_time, beta_list=None):
   a=normalize_sequence(a)
   if max_time <=5: return a
   if beta_list is None:
       beta_list=[0.5,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005]
   n=len(a)
   x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   if np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=x-np.mean(x)
   best_score, _ = internal_score(a)
   best_a=a.copy()
   start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_time
   if n <= 7000: est=0.005
   elif n <= 11000: est=0.012
   else: est=0.025
   for beta in beta_list:
       now=time.monotonic()
       if now > deadline - 5: break
       sc, cmax = internal_score(best_a)
       if not np.isfinite(sc): break
       tau = beta * max(cmax, 1e-15)
       time_rem = deadline - now
       maxfun = int(np.clip(time_rem * 0.35 / est, 30, 1000))
       maxiter = max(20, maxfun // 3)
       try:
           res = minimize(smoothed_objective, x, args=(tau,), jac=smoothed_gradient,
                          method='L-BFGS-B',
                          options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-13,
                                   'gtol': 1e-10, 'maxls': 40, 'disp': False})
           if res is not None and hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
               x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
       except Exception:
           pass
       if np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
           a_cur = softmax(x)
           x_cur = np.log(np.maximum(a_cur,1e-300)) - np.mean(np.log(np.maximum(a_cur,1e-300)))? Better x -= mean(x).
           sc_cur,_=internal_score(a_cur)
           if np.isfinite(sc_cur) and sc_cur < best_score:
               best_score=sc_cur; best_a=a_cur.copy()
               x = np.log(np.maximum(a_cur,1e-300))
       x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       x = x - np.mean(x)
       # optional update x from best_a if improved? x from result may be worse shape; but next beta using x may not be best. Use x from best_a if best_a updated? We can set x=log(best_a) if best improved or if result bad? Maybe use result x because smooth path. But if result score worse, using it might hurt. We can choose: if sc_cur < current_x_score? Track. Simpler: keep x from result if finite; L-BFGS from current shape. But if objective with new tau, starting from current x (maybe not best) okay. To ensure best, we could after each beta set x=log(best_a) to always continue from best. But beta annealing from best good. Do that.
       x = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300)); x = x - np.mean(x)
   return best_a
```
This may miss progress if result x better than best until final exact evaluation? We evaluate after each L-BFGS and update. If result not better exact but smoother path might with lower beta lead to better? Starting from best maybe safe. I think continue from best exact. Good.

But if best_a has high max? L-BFGS objective smooth, starting from best exact. Fine.

`polish`:
```
def polish(a, max_time):
    a=normalize_sequence(a)
    if max_time <= 5: return a
    best_score,_=internal_score(a); best=a.copy()
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_time
    eps_list=[0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.0]
    change_list=[0.03,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001]
    for eps in eps_list:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
        current=a.copy()
        for _ in range(30):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            c=convolution(current); M=float(c.max()) if c.size else 0
            if M <= 0: break
            if eps > 0:
                tau=eps*M
                shifted=(c-M)/tau
                w=np.exp(shifted); ws=w.sum()
                if ws>0 and np.isfinite(ws): w=w/ws
                else: w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
            else:
                thresh=float(np.percentile(c, 98)) if c.size else 0
                w=(c >= thresh).astype(float)
                ws=w.sum()
                if ws>0: w=w/ws
                else: w=np.ones_like(c)/len(c)
            ga=cross_convolution(w, current)
            gmax=float(np.max(np.abs(ga)))
            if gmax <= 0: break
            typical=max(1.0/len(current), float(np.mean(current)))
            improved=False
            for change in change_list:
                step=change*typical/gmax
                a2=current - step*ga
                a2=np.maximum(a2,0)
                s=a2.sum()
                if s<=0: continue
                a2/=s
                sc,_=internal_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score=sc; best=a2.copy(); current=a2; improved=True; break
            if not improved:
                break
    return best
```
This can be slow due `internal_score` inside loop recomputes conv; but small.

`optimize_length`:
```
def optimize_length(n, max_time, base_seq=None):
    n=max(64,int(n))
    if max_time <= 10:
        return central_sequence(n)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max_time
    seeds=[]
    central=central_sequence(n)
    seeds.append(central)
    if base_seq is not None and len(base_seq)>0:
        bseq=resample_sequence(base_seq,n)
        seeds.append(bseq)
    # add a right-skewed central if time enough and not duplicate
    if max_time > 500:
       rc=central_sequence(n)[::-1]
       seeds.append(normalize_sequence(rc))
    screened=[]
    screen_budget = max(20, min(180, max_time*0.15))
    screen_deadline=min(deadline - max_time*0.7, start+screen_budget)
    # If max_time small, screen_budget smaller
    for idx, seed in enumerate(seeds):
        if time.monotonic() > screen_deadline:
           cand=seed
        else:

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))**2.

Strategy:
- Work both with normalized sequences and with unnormalized sequences scaled
  so that their convolution peak is 1.
- Use central-binomial seeding (the asymptotic truncated square-root of a flat
  convolution) and resampled incumbent seeds.
- Apply a fast FFT-based deconvolution/Newton-like update that tries to raise
  the under-flat convolution lags while keeping the peak at 1.
- Polish with L-BFGS on a log-sum-exp smoothed max-convolution objective.
- Finish with a small exact subgradient projection polish.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_CONV_L_CACHE = {}


def _conv_len(n):
    """Smallest power-of-two FFT length for linear convolution of length n."""
    L = _CONV_L_CACHE.get(n)
    if L is None:
        m = 2 * n - 1
        L = 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
        _CONV_L_CACHE[n] = L
    return L


def softmax(x):
    """Numerically stable softmax."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    xm = float(x.max())
    if not np.isfinite(xm):
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=float) / x.size
    e = np.exp(x - xm)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=float) / x.size
    return e / s


def normalize_sequence(a):
    """Clip to non-negative and normalize sum to one."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=float) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def convolution(a):
    """Linear convolution a*a, using direct for tiny lengths and FFT otherwise."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if n <= 512:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        L = _conv_len(n)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
        c = c[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c


def cross_convolution(w, a):
    """
    Return 2 * grad_a sum_k w_k c_k, where c = a * a.

    If c_k = sum_i a_i a_{k-i}, then
    grad_i = 2 * sum_j a_j w_{i+j}.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    L = _conv_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * W, L)[:n]
    h = np.asarray(h.real, dtype=float)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return 2.0 * h


def internal_score(a):
    """Internal score using FFT convolution. Returns (score, max_coeff)."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    c = convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        m = 0.0
    else:
        m = float(c.max())
    return 2.0 * n * m, m


def score_unnormalized(b):
    """Score of arbitrary scale; equivalent to best homogeneous scaling."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return float("inf")
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolution(b)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if m <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return 2.0 * int(b.size) * m / (s * s)


def scale_max_one(b):
    """Scale b so max(b*b)=1; also return scaled convolution."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    c = convolution(b)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return b.copy(), 1.0, np.zeros_like(c)
    return b / np.sqrt(m), 1.0, c / m


def central_sequence(n):
    """
    Truncated coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2): binom(2i,i)/4^i.
    Its first half self-convolution is exactly flat (up to roundoff).
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    b[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] * (2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i)
    return normalize_sequence(b)


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    """Linear resample an incumbent sequence into a new length n."""
    seq = np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
    seq = np.nan_to_num(seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    seq = np.maximum(seq, 0.0)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    if seq.size == n:
        return normalize_sequence(seq)
    if seq.size == 1:
        return normalize_sequence(np.full(n, max(float(seq[0]), 0.0)))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, seq.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, seq)
    return normalize_sequence(out)


def deconv_step(b, c_scaled, target, reg_frac):
    """
    One regularized polynomial-deconvolution step.

    Solves approximately B q = (target - B*B)/(2B) in the frequency domain,
    where B is the DFT of b and c_scaled is the normalized convolution peak.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=float)
    c_scaled = np.asarray(c_scaled, dtype=float)
    n = int(b.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    L = _conv_len(n)
    R = target - c_scaled
    R = np.nan_to_num(R, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    Rf = np.fft.rfft(R, L)
    denom = np.abs(B) ** 2
    maxden = float(np.max(denom)) if denom.size else 1.0
    if maxden <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxden):
        maxden = 1.0
    reg = reg_frac * maxden
    Qf = 0.5 * np.conj(B) * Rf / (denom + reg)
    q = np.fft.irfft(Qf, L)[:n]
    q = np.asarray(q.real, dtype=float)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return q


def deconvolve(a, max_time):
    """
    Improve a sequence in the unnormalized objective:
      maximize sum(b) subject to max(coeff(b*b)) <= 1.
    Repeatedly solve a regularized deconvolution toward a flat coefficient
    target and accept only moves that lower the homogeneous score.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or max_time <= 0:
        return a

    c0 = convolution(a)
    m0 = float(c0.max()) if c0.size else 0.0
    if m0 <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m0):
        return a

    b = a / np.sqrt(m0)
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_score = score_unnormalized(b)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        return a

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max_time)

    targets = [1.0, 1.05, 0.95, 1.02, 0.98]
    reg_fracs = [1e-9, 1e-7, 1e-5]
    deltas = [0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.40]

    ti = 0
    ri = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        b1, _, c1 = scale_max_one(best_b)
        target = targets[ti % len(targets)]
        reg = reg_fracs[ri % len(reg_fracs)]

        q = deconv_step(b1, c1, target, reg)
        qmax = float(np.max(np.abs(q))) if q.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(qmax) or qmax <= 1e-15:
            ti += 1
            if ti % 5 == 0:
                ri += 1
            continue

        best_sc = best_score
        best_cand = None
        signs = [1] if stall <= 1 else [1, -1]

        for sign in signs:
            for delta in deltas:
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                alpha = (sign * delta) / qmax
                b2 = b1 + alpha * q
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(b2)):
                    continue
                if np.any(b2 < 0.0):
                    b2 = np.maximum(b2, 0.0)
                s2 = float(b2.sum())
                if s2 <= 0.0:
                    continue
                if float(np.max(b2)) > 10.0:
                    continue
                sc2 = score_unnormalized(b2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc2
                    best_cand = b2.copy()

        if best_cand is not None:
            best_b = best_cand
            best_score = best_sc
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 5:
                break

        ti += 1
        if stall == 0 and (ti % 3 == 0):
            ri += 1

    return normalize_sequence(best_b)
``` 
```
def smoothed_objective(x, tau):
    """LSE-smoothed max of normalized a*a, where a=softmax(x)."""
    a = softmax(x)
    c = convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return 0.0
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    e = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e100
    return cmax + tau * np.log(s) - np.log(float(c.size))


def smoothed_gradient(x, tau):
    """Gradient of smoothed_objective with respect to log-softmax variables."""
    a = softmax(x)
    c = convolution(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    if cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(a)
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
    else:
        w = w / ws
    ga = cross_convolution(w, a)
    dot = float(np.dot(a, ga))
    gx = a * (ga - dot)
    gx = np.nan_to_num(gx, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return gx


def run_lbfgs(a, max_time, beta_list=None):
    """
    L-BFGS polishing of the LSE-smoothed max-convolution objective.
    beta is the smoothing relative to current peak: tau = beta * peak.
    """
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    if max_time <= 5.0:
        return a
    if beta_list is None:
        beta_list = [0.5, 0.2, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005]

    n = int(a.size)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    x = x - np.mean(x)

    best_score, _ = internal_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 1e100
    best_a = a.copy()

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max_time)

    # Rough estimate of one objective+gradient cost in seconds.
    if n <= 7000:
        est = 0.005
    elif n <= 11000:
        est = 0.012
    else:
        est = 0.025

    for beta in beta_list:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 5.0:
            break

        sc_cur, cmax = internal_score(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc_cur):
            break
        tau = beta * max(float(cmax), 1e-15)
        if tau <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(tau):
            continue

        time_rem = max(1.0, deadline - now)
        maxfun = int(np.clip(time_rem * 0.35 / est, 30, 1000))
        maxiter = max(20, maxfun // 3)

        trial_x = x
        try:
            res = minimize(
                smoothed_objective,
                x,
                args=(tau,),
                jac=smoothed_gradient,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-13,
                    "gtol": 1e-10,
                    "maxls": 40,
                    "disp": False,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                trial_x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
        except Exception:
            trial_x = x

        trial_x = np.nan_to_num(trial_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        a_cur = softmax(trial_x)
        sc_trial, _ = internal_score(a_cur)

        if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial < best_score:
            best_score = sc_trial
            best_a = a_cur.copy()

        # Usually continue the smooth annealing from the trial point, unless it
        # has become clearly worse in the exact objective.
        if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial <= best_score + 0.01:
            x = trial_x
        else:
            x = np.log(np.maximum(best_a, 1e-300))
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        x = x - np.mean(x)

    return best_a


def polish_subgradient(a, max_time):
    """Short exact subgradient projection polish with adaptive active weights."""
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    if max_time <= 5.0:
        return a

    best_score, _ = internal_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max_time)

    eps_list = [0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.0]
    change_list = [0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001]

    for eps in eps_list:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        current = a.copy()
        for _ in range(30):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            c = convolution(current)
            if c.size == 0:
                break
            M = float(c.max())
            if M <= 0.0:
                break

            if eps > 0.0:
                tau = eps * M
                shifted = (c - M) / tau
                w = np.exp(shifted)
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                    w = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
                else:
                    w = w / ws
            else:
                thresh = float(np.percentile(c, 98))
                w = (c >= thresh).astype(float)
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0:
                    w = np.ones_like(c) / c.size
                else:
                    w = w / ws

            ga = cross_convolution(w, current)
            gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(ga))) if ga.size else 0.0
            if gmax <= 0.0:
                break

            typical = max(1.0 / float(current.size), float(np.mean(current)))
            improved = False
            for change in change_list:
                step = change * typical / gmax
                a2 = current - step * ga
                a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0.0)
                s2 = float(a2.sum())
                if s2 <= 0.0:
                    continue
                a2 = a2 / s2
                sc2, _ = internal_score(a2)
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_a = a2.copy()
                    current = a2
                    improved = True
                    break
            if not improved:
                break

    return best_a


def optimize_length(n, max_time, base_seq=None):
    """
    Run the full search for a fixed length: seed screening, deconvolution,
    L-BFGS smoothing, a second smoother, and final polish.
    """
    n = int(max(64, n))
    if max_time <= 15.0:
        return central_sequence(n)

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(max_time)

    seeds = [central_sequence(n)]
    if base_seq is not None and len(base_seq) > 0:
        seeds.append(resample_sequence(base_seq, n))

    screen_budget = max(20.0, min(180.0, 0.16 * max_time))
    screen_deadline = min(deadline - 0.70 * max_time, start + screen_budget)

    screened = []
    for seed in seeds:
        if time.monotonic() >= screen_deadline:
            cand = seed
        else:
            t_left = max(0.0, screen_deadline - time.monotonic())
            t = max(5.0, min(70.0, t_left))
            if t < 5.0:
                cand = seed
            else:
                try:
                    cand = deconvolve(seed, t)
                except Exception:
                    cand = seed
        sc, _ = internal_score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            screened.append((sc, cand))
        else:
            screened.append((1e100, cand))

    if not screened:
        central = central_sequence(n)
        sc, _ = internal_score(central)
        screened.append((sc, central))

    screened.sort()

    if max_time > 900.0 and len(screened) > 1:
        chain_seeds = [screened[0][1], screened[1][1]]
    else:
        chain_seeds = [screened[0][1]]

    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left <= 35.0:
        return normalize_sequence(screened[0][1])

    time_per = time_left / float(len(chain_seeds))
    best_a = None
    best_score = 1e100

    for idx, seed in enumerate(chain_seeds):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        t_remaining = max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic())

        # Allocate stages within this chain.
        t1 = 0.50 * t_remaining
        t2 = 0.20 * t_remaining
        t3 = 0.20 * t_remaining
        t4 = 0.10 * t_remaining
        total = t1 + t2 + t3 + t4
        if total > t_remaining:
            scale = t_remaining / total
            t1 *= scale
            t2 *= scale
            t3 *= scale
            t4 *= scale

        a = seed.copy()

        if t1 > 25.0:
            try:
                a = run_lbfgs(a, t1)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if t2 > 12.0:
            try:
                a = deconvolve(a, t2)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if t3 > 25.0:
            low_betas = [0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005]
            try:
                a = run_lbfgs(a, t3, beta_list=low_betas)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if t4 > 12.0:
            try:
                a = polish_subgradient(a, t4)
            except Exception:
                pass

        sc, _ = internal_score(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a

    if best_a is not None:
        return normalize_sequence(best_a)
    return normalize_sequence(screened[0][1])


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Main entry point. Returns a list of non-negative heights."""
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 60.0)

    base_seq = None
    if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
        try:
            base_seq = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
            base_seq = np.nan_to_num(base_seq, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            base_seq = np.maximum(base_seq, 0.0)
        except Exception:
            base_seq = None

    base_len = 0
    if base_seq is not None and base_seq.size > 32:
        base_len = int(base_seq.size)
    if base_len <= 0 or base_len > 16384:
        base_len = 4806

    # Choose a few lengths. Keep the final length modest so the external
    # np.convolve in evaluate_sequence remains comfortably fast.
    lengths = []
    if 512 <= base_len <= 16384:
        lengths.append(base_len)
    else:
        lengths.append(4806)

    for l in (12288, 8192, 4806):
        if l not in lengths and len(lengths) < 3:
            lengths.append(l)

    if len(lengths) == 1:
        weights = [1.0]
    elif len(lengths) == 2:
        weights = [0.58, 0.42]
    else:
        weights = [0.48, 0.34, 0.18]

    total_budget = max(60.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    results = []
    best_a = None
    best_score = 1e100

    for idx, n in enumerate(lengths):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        t = min(weights[idx] * total_budget, deadline - time.monotonic())
        if t < 45.0:
            break
        try:
            a = optimize_length(int(n), float(t), base_seq)
            sc, _ = internal_score(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                results.append((sc, a))
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a
                print(f"internal score n={n}: {sc:.7f} elapsed={time.monotonic()-start:.1f}")
            else:
                fb = central_sequence(int(n))
                fb_sc, _ = internal_score(fb)
                results.append((fb_sc, fb))
                if fb_sc < best_score:
                    best_score = fb_sc
                    best_a = fb
        except Exception:
            print(f"failed length n={n}")

    if best_a is None:
        if base_seq is not None and base_seq.size > 0:
            best_a = normalize_sequence(base_seq)
        else:
            best_a = central_sequence(4806)

    eval_list = list(results)
    if base_seq is not None and base_seq.size > 0:
        base_a = normalize_sequence(base_seq)
        base_sc, _ = internal_score(base_a)
        if np.isfinite(base_sc):
            eval_list.append((base_sc, base_a))

    eval_list.sort()

    final_best = best_a
    final_best_score = best_score

    # Verify a few best internal candidates with the exact external evaluator.
    for sc_internal, cand in eval_list[:8]:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            seq = [float(v) for v in normalize_sequence(cand)]
            sc_exact = evaluate_sequence(seq)
            if np.isfinite(sc_exact) and sc_exact < final_best_score:
                final_best_score = sc_exact
                final_best = cand
                print(f"exact external score candidate: {sc_exact:.7f}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = normalize_sequence(final_best)
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(out.size, dtype=float) / max(1, out.size)
        s = 1.0
    out = out / s

    try:
        print(f"final internal candidate score: {internal_score(out)[0]:.7f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(v) for v in out]
```
